發票樂透|invoice lottery*Receipt lottery system implemented in Taiwan to encourage citizens to collect and register their invoices from purchases. Citizens are incentivized to do so because drawings are held every 2 months and they can win cash prizes if the numbers on their invoices match the winning number in this lottery. The govenrment holds these lotteries to encourage purchase visibility in order to combat tax evasion.|fā piào lè tòu|Taiwan's invoices are like the lottery. You can check your numbers every two months to see if you won a prize.|台灣的發票像樂透一樣，每兩個月可以對獎。

QQ|*cries* *it looks like a crying face|QQ|*cries* hmm… I live an expensive lifestyle right now, if the pay’s not great then I have to think about it.|QQ 我現在生活開銷頗貴，薪水不高我要考慮一下。
